<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The letters on the $7$ cards below have a number $1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$ or $7$ on the back, **but not in that order**.

$\large{\boxed{S} \ \boxed{t} \ \boxed{P} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{u} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{s}}$

If $\boxed{s}$  has the number $1$ one the back and the cards

$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to $8$,

$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $9$,

$\boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $11$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The letters on the $7$ cards below have a number $1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$ or $7$ on the back, **but not in that order**.
<emptyline>
$\large{\boxed{S} \ \boxed{t} \ \boxed{P} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{u} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{s}}$
<emptyline>
If $\boxed{s}$  has the number $1$ one the back and the cards
<emptyline>
$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to $8$,
<emptyline>
$\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $9$,
<emptyline>
$\boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{P}$ add up to $11$.
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$\begin{aligned}
s + a + t &= 8 && (1) \\\\
s + a + P &= 9 && (2) \\\\
l + a + P &= 11 && (3)
\end{aligned}$

Subtracting equation $2$ from $3$,

$\begin{aligned}
(l + a + P) - (s + a + P) &= 11 - 9 \\\\
l - s &= 2 \\\\
l - 1 &= 2 \\\\
l &= 3
\end{aligned}$

Use equation $1$ to find the sum:

$\begin{aligned}
s + a + l + t &= (s + a + t) + l \\\\
&= 8 + 3 \\\\
&= 11
\end{aligned}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{P}  \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What does $\boxed{s} \ \boxed{P}  \ \boxed{l} \ \boxed{a} \ \boxed{t}$ add up to?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the previous part, we know $s=1$ and $l=3$.

Using the given equations to find expressions for $P$ and $t$:

$\begin{aligned}
s + a + P &= 9 \\
1 + a + P &= 9 \\
a + P &= 8
\end{aligned}$

$\begin{aligned}
s + a + t &= 8 \\
1 + a + t &= 8 \\
a + t &= 7
\end{aligned}$

Since the cards have distinct numbers from $1$ to $7$:
We must find an integer $a$ such that $P$ and $t$ are also distinct integers from $1$ to $7$, and none equal $1$ ($s$) or $3$ ($l$).

If $a=2$:
$\begin{aligned}
P &= 8 - 2 = 6 \\
t &= 7 - 2 = 5
\end{aligned}$
The set $\{1, 2, 3, 5, 6\}$ contains distinct values. This is a valid solution.

(Note: Other values for $a$ fail. For example, if $a=6$, then $t=1$, which duplicates $s$).

Now calculating the required sum:
$\begin{aligned}
&= s + P + l + a + t \\
&= 1 + 6 + 3 + 2 + 5 \\
&= 17
\end{aligned}$

Subtract equation $2$ from $1$

$
\begin{aligned}
s + a + p - (s + a + t)       &= 9 - 8 \\\\
p - t                         &= 1 \\\\
p - 1                         &= t \\\\
t                             &= p - 1 \\\\
l + a + p                     &= 11 \\\\
3 + a + p                     &= 11 \\\\
a + p                         &= 11 - 3 \\\\
a + p                         &= 8 \\\\
a                             &= 8 - p \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Substitute $a$ and $p$ in equation $1$

$
\begin{aligned}
s + a + t                       &= 8 \\\\
1 + 8 - p + p - 1               &= 8 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Five Whatsits and four Thingummyjigs cost $\pounds 14.95$. Work out the cost of a Whatsit in each of the following cases: 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When twelve Thingummyjigs cost $\pounds 10.20$. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When twelve Thingummyjigs cost $\pounds 10.20$. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
12b                                                       &= 10.20 \\\\
\dfrac{12b}{3}                                            &= \dfrac{10.20}{3} \\\\
\dfrac{4 \times 3 \times b}{3}                            &= \dfrac{3.4 \times 3}{3} \\\\
\dfrac{4 \times \cancel{3} \times b}{\cancel{3}}          &= \dfrac{3.4 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
4b                                                        &= 3.4
\end{aligned}
$

Let's substitute the value of $4b$ in first equation.

$
\begin{aligned}
5a + 4b           &= 14.95 \\\\
5a + 3.4          &= 14.95 \\\\
5a                &= 11.55 \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac{11.55}{5} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac{2.31 \times 5}{5} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac{2.31 \times \cancel{5}}{\cancel{5}} \\\\
a                 &= \pounds 2.31 \\\\
4b                &= 3.4 \\\\ 
b                 &= \dfrac{0.85 \times 4}{4} \\\\ 
b                 &= \dfrac{0.85 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\ 
b                 &= \pounds 0.85
\end{aligned}
$

The cost of Whatsits $= \pounds 2.31$

The cost of Thingummyjigs $= \pounds 0.85$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 2.31$ and $\pounds 0.85$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When a Thingummyjig costs twice as much as a Whatsit.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When a Thingummyjig costs twice as much as a Whatsit.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$b = 2a$

Let's substitute the value of $b$ in first equation.

$
\begin{aligned}
5a + 4b                    &= 14.95 \\\\
5a + 4 \times 2a           &= 14.95 \\\\
5a + 8a                    &= 14.95 \\\\
13a                        &= 14.95 \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac{14.95}{13} \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac{1.15 \times 13}{13} \\\\
a                          &= \dfrac{1.15 \times \cancel{13}}{\cancel{13}} \\\\
a                          &= \pounds 1.15 \\\\
b                          &= 2a \\\\
b                          &= 2 \times 1.15 \\\\
b                          &= \pounds 2.3
\end{aligned}
$

The cost of Whatsits $= \pounds 1.15$

The cost of Thingummyjigs $= \pounds 2.3$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\pounds 1.15$ and $\pounds 2.3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Elizabeth writes down:

One multiple of $13$ and two different factors of $77$.

Elizabeth adds up her three numbers. Her answer is greater than fifty but less than sixty.

What three numbers could Elizabeth have written down?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Elizabeth writes down:
<emptyline>
One multiple of $13$ and two different factors of $77$.
<emptyline>
Elizabeth adds up her three numbers. Her answer is greater than fifty but less than sixty.
<emptyline>
What three numbers could Elizabeth have written down?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the two factors of 77 $= a, b$

Let the multiple of 13 $= c$

As per the question,

50 < a + b + c < 60

Factors of $77 = 1, 7, 11, 77$

We can easily infer the values by using trial and error. Lets find out the value of a, b and c which satisfies the condition above.

We can ignore 77 as it is greater than 60.

|  c   |    a,b    |          sum           |  50 < sum < 60  |
|:----:|:---------:|:----------------------:|:---------------:|
| $13$ |  $1,7$    |  $13 + 1 + 7 = 21$     |       No        |
| $13$ |  $1,11$   |  $13 + 1 + 11 = 25$    |       No        |
| $13$ |  $7,11$   |  $13 + 7 + 11 = 31$    |       No        |
| $26$ |  $1,7$    |  $26 + 1 + 7 = 34$     |       No        |
| $26$ |  $1,11$   |  $26 + 1 + 11 = 38$    |       No        |
| $26$ |  $7,11$   |  $26 + 7 + 11 = 44$    |       No        |
| $39$ |  $1,7$    |  $39 + 1 + 7 = 47$     |       No        |
| $39$ |  $1,11$   |  $39 + 1 + 11 = 51$    |       Yes       |
| $39$ |  $7,11$   |  $39 + 7 + 11 = 57$    |       Yes       |

A quick check shows that $a = 1,b = 11,c = 39$ or $a = 7,b = 11,c = 39$ meets all the criteria above.

Numbers Elizabeth could have written down $= 1,11,39$ or $7,11,39$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1,11,39$ or $7,11,39$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the two factors of 77 $= a, b$
<emptyline>
Let the multiple of 13 $= c$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
50 < a + b + c < 60
<emptyline>
Factors of $77 = 1, 7, 11, 77$
<emptyline>
We can easily infer the values by using trial and error. Lets find out the value of a, b and c which satisfies the condition above.
<emptyline>
We can ignore 77 as it is greater than 60.
<emptyline>
|  c   |    a,b    |          sum           |  50 < sum < 60  |
|:----:|:---------:|:----------------------:|:---------------:|
| $13$ |  $1,7$    |  $13 + 1 + 7 = 21$     |       No        |
| $13$ |  $1,11$   |  $13 + 1 + 11 = 25$    |       No        |
| $13$ |  $7,11$   |  $13 + 7 + 11 = 31$    |       No        |
| $26$ |  $1,7$    |  $26 + 1 + 7 = 34$     |       No        |
| $26$ |  $1,11$   |  $26 + 1 + 11 = 38$    |       No        |
| $26$ |  $7,11$   |  $26 + 7 + 11 = 44$    |       No        |
| $39$ |  $1,7$    |  $39 + 1 + 7 = 47$     |       No        |
| $39$ |  $1,11$   |  $39 + 1 + 11 = 51$    |       Yes       |
| $39$ |  $7,11$   |  $39 + 7 + 11 = 57$    |       Yes       |
<emptyline>
A quick check shows that $a = 1,b = 11,c = 39$ or $a = 7,b = 11,c = 39$ meets all the criteria above.
<emptyline>
Numbers Elizabeth could have written down $= 1,11,39$ or $7,11,39$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

How many toffees did Detti get?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many toffees did Detti get?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

TODO: Its easier to solve it backwards. look at generated answers for details

Let the number of toffees $= a$

As per the question,

After eating $1$ and dividing it equally with emily, toffees left $= b = \dfrac{(a-1)}{2}$

Again after eating one and dividing it equally with Detti, toffees left $= c = \dfrac{b-1}{2}$

After eating one and giving last to Sean, toffees left $= c - 1 -1 = 0$

So,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Initial toffees}  &= a \\\\
\text{After eating 1}  &= a - 1 \\\\
\text{Shared with Emily} &= \dfrac{a - 1}{2} \\\\
\text{After eating 1 more}  &= \dfrac{a - 1}{2} - 1 \\\\
\text{Shared with Detti} &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times \left(\dfrac{a - 1}{2} - 1\right) \\\\
\text{After eating 1}  &= c - 1 \\\\
\text{Given to Sean} &= c - 1 - 1 = 0 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Initial toffees}  &= a \\\\
\text{After eating 1}  &= a - 1 \\\\
\text{Shared with Emily} &= \dfrac{a - 1}{2} \\\\
\text{After eating 1 more}  &= \dfrac{a - 1}{2} - 1 \\\\
\text{Shared with Detti} &= \dfrac{1}{2} \times \left(\dfrac{a - 1}{2} - 1\right) \\\\
\text{After eating 1}  &= c - 1 \\\\
\text{Given to Sean} &= c - 1 - 1 = 0 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$

Number of toffees detti got $= 2$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2$

</div>
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Number of toffees Emily got $= b$

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{b-1}{2} &= c \\\\
\dfrac{b-1}{2} &= 2 \\\\
b - 1          &= 2 \times 2 \\\\
b - 1          &= 4 \\\\
b              &= 4 + 1 \\\\
b              &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

Number of toffees Emily got $= 5$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$5$

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Number of toffees Billy had $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac{a-1}{2}  &= b \\\\
\dfrac{a-1}{2}  &= 5 \\\\
a - 1           &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
a - 1           &= 10 \\\\
a               &= 11 
\end{aligned}
$

Number toffees in the start $= 11$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11$

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If Pia keeps her cards in the order $A \ B \ C$ and starts with $11$, what is her answer?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If Pia keeps her cards in the order $A \ B \ C$ and starts with $11$, what is her answer?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= ((11 \times 5) + 3) \div 2 \\\\
&= (55 + 3) \div 2 \\\\
&= 58 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{58}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{29 \times 2}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{29 \times  \cancel 2}{\cancel 2} \\\\
&= 29
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$29$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What whole number would she need to start with to get an answer of $19$, if 
her cards were still arranged in the order $A \ B \ C$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What whole number would she need to start with to get an answer of $19$, if 
her cards were still arranged in the order $A \ B \ C$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the starting number $= a$

As per the question, 

$
\begin{aligned}
((a \times 5) + 3) \div 2 &= 19 \\\\
(a \times 5) + 3          &= 19 \times 2 \\\\
(a \times 5) + 3          &= 38 \\\\
a \times 5                &= 38 - 3 \\\\
a \times 5                &= 35 \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {35} {5} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {7 \times 5} {5} \\\\
a                         &= \dfrac {7 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
a                         &= 7
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If Pia arranges her cards in the order $C \ B \ A$ and starts with number $4$, what is her answer?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If Pia arranges her cards in the order $C \ B \ A$ and starts with number $4$, what is her answer?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= ((4 \div 2) + 3) \times 5 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{4}{2} + 3) \times 5 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{2 \times 2}{2} + 3) \times 5 \\\\
&= (\dfrac{2 \times \cancel 2}{\cancel 2} + 3) \times 5 \\\\
&= (2 + 3) \times 5 \\\\
&= 5 \times 5 \\\\
&= 25
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$25$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Pia changes the order of the cards. She starts with a whole number and 
ends up with $15$. Give all the possible orders in which she could have used her three cards.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Pia changes the order of the cards. She starts with a whole number and 
ends up with $15$. Give all the possible orders in which she could have used her three cards.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Possible arrangement are $= \text{BAC, BCA,  CBA}$

Let's verify,

Let the whole number $= a$

$\text{BAC} \rightarrow $

$
\begin{aligned}
((a + 3) \times 5) \div 2      &= 15 \\\\
(a + 3) \times \dfrac{5}{2}    &= 15 \\\\
(a + 3) \times 5               &= 15 \times 2 \\\\
(a + 3) \times 5               &= 30 \\\\
5a + 15                        &= 30 \\\\
5a                             &= 30 - 15 \\\\
5a                             &= 15 \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{15}{5} \\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{5 \times 3}{5}\\\\
a                              &= \dfrac{\cancel 5 \times 3}{\cancel 5}\\\\
a                              &= 5 
\end{aligned}
$

$\text{BCA} \rightarrow $

$
\begin{aligned}
(((a + 3) \div 2 ) \times 5 )  &= 15 \\\\
(a + 3) \div 2                 &= \dfrac{15}{5} \\\\
(a + 3) \div 2                 &= \dfrac{5 \times 3}{5} \\\\
(a + 3) \div 2                 &= \dfrac{\cancel 5 \times 3}{\cancel 5} \\\\
(a + 3) \div 2                 &= 3 \\\\
(a + 3)                        &= 3 \times 2 \\\\
a + 3                          &= 6 \\\\
a                              &= 6 - 3 \\\\
a                              &= 3                    
\end{aligned}
$

$\text{CBA} \rightarrow $ 

$
\begin{aligned}
(((a \div 2 ) + 3) \times 5 )   &= 15 \\\\
(a \div 2 ) + 3                 &= \dfrac{15}{5} \\\\
(a \div 2 ) + 3                 &= \dfrac{5 \times 3}{5} \\\\
(a \div 2 ) + 3                 &= \dfrac{\cancel 5 \times 3}{\cancel 5} \\\\
(a \div 2 ) + 3                 &= 3 \\\\
a \div 2                        &= 3 - 3 \\\\
a \div 2                        &= 0 \\\\
a                               &= 0 \times 2 \\\\
a                               &= 0
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{BAC, BCA,  CBA}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here is an example of a function machine:

Rule: Multiply by three, then subtract $2$.

Fill in the gaps in this new function machine:

![papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q10-i01.png](/assets/papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q10-i01.png "papers/city-of-london-school-for-girls/sample/sample-paper-1/s02-q10-i01.png")

<div class="workings">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Rule: Multiply by 4, then subtract 8.

Let the first missing number $= a$

Let the second missing number $= b$

Let the third missing number $= c$

As per the question

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{First missing number}         &= 10 \times 4 - 8 \\\\
a                                   &= 40 - 8 \\\\
a                                   &= 32
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the rule to find b.

$
\begin{aligned}
4b - 8          &= 44 \\\\
4b              &= 44 + 8 \\\\
b               &= \dfrac {52} {4} \\\\
b               &= \dfrac {4 \times 13} {4} \\\\
b               &= \dfrac {\cancel 4 \times 13} {\cancel 4} \\\\
b               &= 13
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the rule to find c.

$
\begin{aligned}
4c - 8          &= 2 \\\\
4c              &= 2 + 8 \\\\
c               &= \dfrac {10} {4} \\\\
c               &= \dfrac {10 \times 25} {4 \times 25} \\\\
c               &= \dfrac {250} {100} \\\\
c               &= 2.5
\end{aligned}
$

Missing numbers $= 32, 13, 2.5$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Missing numbers $= 32, 13, 2.5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The centre pages of a book are numbered $34$ and $35$. How many pages are in the book?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The centre pages of a book are numbered $34$ and $35$. How many pages are in the book?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of pages $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a - 1} {2}          &= 34 \\\\
a - 1                       &= 34 \times 2 \\\\
a - 1                       &= 68 \\\\
a                           &= 68 + 1 \\\\
a                           &= 69
\end{aligned}
$

Number of pages in the book $= 69$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$69$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

*ABBR: TODO:: Find the right katex symbol.*

Each shape in this grid is hiding a particular number so that the three shapes in any row or column add up to the value written 
at the end of that row or column. 

|  $\spadesuit$|   Sun        |   Sun       | $16$ |
|:------------:|:------------:|:-----------:|:----:|
| $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$| $12$ |
| $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$| $18$ |
| $\spadesuit$ | $14$         |   $?$       |  $+$ |

![missing image](/assets/papers/missing/missing_image.svg "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-a/s01-q15-i00.png")

Work out the number that should replace the question mark.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">rtq_abbr_working_katex_show_correct_symbol

<emptyline>

|:------------:|:------------:|:-----------:|:----:|

| $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$ | $\spadesuit$| $18$ |
| $\spadesuit$ | $14$         |   $?$       |  $+$ |
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
Work out the number that should replace the question mark.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the value of diamond, sun and club $= d, s, c$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
d + s + s           &= 16 \\\\
d + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
d + d + d           &= 12 \\\\
3d                  &= 12 \\\\
c + d + c           &= 18 \\\\
2c + d              &= 18
\end{aligned}
$

Let's find the value of d.

$
\begin{aligned}
3d                  &= 12 \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {12} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {3} \\\\
d                   &= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 4} {\cancel 3} \\\\
d                   &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of d to find s.

$
\begin{aligned}
4 + 2s              &= 16 \\\\
2s                  &= 16 - 4 \\\\
s                   &= \dfrac {12} {2} \\\\
s                   &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the value of d to find c.

$
\begin{aligned}
2c + 4              &= 18 \\\\
2c                  &= 18 - 4 \\\\
c                   &= \dfrac {14} {2} \\\\
c                   &= 7
\end{aligned}
$

The value of diamond $= 4$

The value of sun $= 6$

The value of club $= 7$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Missing value}            &= s + d + c \\\\
                                &= 6 + 4 + 7 \\\\
                                &= 17
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Work out what time it is now if the time that has passed since nine o'clock this morning is five times as much as the time that is left before eleven o' clock this morning.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Work out what time it is now if the time that has passed since nine o'clock this morning is five times as much as the time that is left before eleven o' clock this morning.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the time left before eleven o'clock is $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time passed since nine o'clock}  &= 5 \times a \\\\
                                        &= 5a
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total time}  &= (11 - 9) \ \text {hours} \\\\
5a + a              &= 2 \ \text{hours} \\\\
6a                  &= (2 \times 60) \ \text {minutes} \\\\
6a                  &= 120 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {120} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times \ 20} {\cancel 6} \\\\
a                   &= 20 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

So, 20 minutes before 11 o'clock $= 10 {:} 40$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10 {:} 40$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the time left before eleven o'clock is $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time passed since nine o'clock}  &= 5 \times a \\\\
                                        &= 5a
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total time}  &= (11 - 9) \ \text {hours} \\\\
5a + a              &= 2 \ \text{hours} \\\\
6a                  &= (2 \times 60) \ \text {minutes} \\\\
6a                  &= 120 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {120} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {6 \times 20} {6} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel 6 \times \ 20} {\cancel 6} \\\\
a                   &= 20 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
So, 20 minutes before 11 o'clock $= 10 {:} 40$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

The following statements are also true:

$
\begin{aligned} 
d \times d &= 121 \\
e \times d \times f &= 1001
\end{aligned}
$

Write down the value of:

$d$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\end{aligned}
$
</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
c \times d \times e          &= 1001 \\\\
7 \times 11 \times e        &= 1001 \\\\
e                           &= \dfrac {1001} {11 \times 7} \\\\
e                           &= \dfrac {11 \times 13 \times 7} {11 \times 7} \\\\
e                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {11} \times 13 \times \cancel 7} {\cancel {11} \times \cancel 7} \\\\
e                           &= 13
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Working**

Prime numbers less than $20 = 2, 3, 5, 7, 11, 13, 17, 19$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + b + c + d + e + f + g + h           &= 2 + 3 + 5 + 7 + 11 + 13 + 17 + 19 \\\\
5 + 3 + 7 + 11 + 13 + f + g + h         &= 67 \\\\
39 + f + g + h                          &= 67 \\\\
f + g + h                               &= 67 - 39 \\\\
f + g + h                               &= 28
\end{aligned}
$

The value of f + g + h $= 28$

**Question**

The cards below have letters on one side, and the numbers $1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$ and $7$ on the other side (but not necessarily in this order on the cards).

\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{L}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}}
}
$

You are told that:

$
\large {
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{L}} \quad
\boxed{\text{P}} \quad
\boxed{\text{H}} \quad
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}}
}
$

adds up to $35$.

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

**Working**

Let the numbers on cards $= p, \ h, \ a, \ b, \ l, \ e, \ t$ 

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + a &= 35 \\\\
28 + a                        &= 35 \\\\
a                             &= 35 - 28 \\\\
a                             &= 7 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

You are also told that:

$
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{L}} \quad
\boxed{\text{P}} \quad
\boxed{\text{H}} \quad
\boxed{\text{A}} 
$ adds up to $20$. 

Work out what the following add up to: 

$
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}} \quad 
\boxed{\text{A}}
$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\boxed{\text{H}} \quad
\boxed{\text{A}} 
$ adds up to $20$. 
<emptyline>
Work out what the following add up to: 
<emptyline>
$
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}} \quad 
\boxed{\text{A}}
$
</code></pre>

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
20 + b + e + t                &= 35 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 35 - 20 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 15 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + a \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + 7 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 22
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The perimeter of this shape is $40 \ \text{units}$. The sides labelled $x$ are all the same length. Find the length of one of these sides.

![papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q20-i00.png](/assets/papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q20-i00.png "papers/dulwich-college/sample/specimen-paper-g/s01-q20-i00.png") 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The perimeter of this shape is $40 \ \text{units}$. The sides labelled $x$ are all the same length. Find the length of one of these sides.
<emptyline>
%image% 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

{/* ![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg) */}
TODOIMAGE



TODO:: Show the diagram with the calculations for sides of the shape which do not have measurements shown.


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


Let the length of side $= a$

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
5 + a + a + a + a + a + 3a + 5 + 2a &= 40 \\\\
10 +  10a                           &= 40 \\\\
10a                                 &= 40 - 10 \\\\
10a                                 &= 30 \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {30} {10} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {3 \times 10} {10} \\\\
a                                   &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel{10}} {\cancel{10}} \\\\
a                                   &= 3 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here is a pattern made from grey and white tiles.

![papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png "papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i00.png")
![papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i01.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i01.png "papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i01.png")
![papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i02.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i02.png "papers/eltham-college/2019/sample-paper-1/s01-q16-i02.png")

A shape in the pattern has $28$ grey tiles.

How many white tiles does it have?

-  $28$
-  $15$
-  $14$
-  $9$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Here is a pattern made from grey and white tiles.
<emptyline>
%image%
%image%
%image%
<emptyline>
A shape in the pattern has $28$ grey tiles.
<emptyline>
How many white tiles does it have?
<emptyline>
-  $28$
-  $15$
-  $14$
-  $9$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{No. of white tiles}                      &= \dfrac {\text{No. of grey tiles}} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {28} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {2 \times 14} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 14} {\cancel{2}} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= 14 + 1 \\\\
                                               &= 15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the diagram,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text{No. of white tiles}                      &= \dfrac {\text{No. of grey tiles}} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {28} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {2 \times 14} {2} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 14} {\cancel{2}} + 1 \\\\
                                               &= 14 + 1 \\\\
                                               &= 15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">B</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Key Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$15$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kiran writes down six different prime numbers $ p, q, r, s, t, u$ all less than $20$, such that:

$p + q = r + s = t + u$

What is the value of $p + q?$

- $16$ 
- $18$ 
- $20$ 
- $24$


</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A country has decided to have just two different coins.
It has been suggested that these should be $3z$ and $5z$ coins.

![papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q15-i00.png](/assets/papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q15-i00.png "papers/eltham-college/sample/sample-paper-4/s01-q15-i00.png")

The shops think this is a good idea since most totals can be made.

For example, a total of $11z$ can be made using $2 \times 3z + 5z.$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

It has been suggested that these should be $3z$ and $5z$ coins.
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Tanya mixes cranberry juice and lemonade to make a refreshing drink.
She uses $3$ times more lemonade than cranberry juice to make the drink.

Tanya uses $759 \ \text{ml}$ of lemonade.

How much of the drink does she make?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Tanya mixes cranberry juice and lemonade to make a refreshing drink.
She uses $3$ times more lemonade than cranberry juice to make the drink.
<emptyline>
Tanya uses $759 \ \text{ml}$ of lemonade.
<emptyline>
How much of the drink does she make?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the amount of cranberry juice used $= a$

Using equivalent ratio,

$
\begin{aligned}
1 \ \text{ml} : 3 \ \text{ml}    &= a \ \text{ml} : 759 \ \text{ml} \\\\
\dfrac{1} {3}                                              &= \dfrac{a} {759} \\\\
a                                                          &= \dfrac{759} {3} \\\\
a                                                          &= \dfrac{3 \times 253} {3} \\\\
a                                                          &= \dfrac{\cancel 3 \times 253} {\cancel 3} \\\\
a                                                          &= 253 \ \text{ml}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total volume of drink}      &= 253 + 759 \\\\
                                  &= 1012 \ \text{ml}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{lemonade}                    &:& \text{cranberry juice} \\\\
= & 3                                  &:& 1 \\\\
= & 1                                  &:& \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
= & 759                                &:& \dfrac {759} {3} \\\\
= & 759                                &:& \dfrac {3 \times 253} {3} \\\\
= & 759                                &:& \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 253} {\cancel 3} \\\\
= & 759 \ \text{ml}                    &:& 253 \ \text{ml}
\end{array}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total volume of drink}      &= 253 + 759 \\\\
                                  &= 1012 \ \text{ml}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1012 \ \text{ml}$

</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A drink is made by mixing fruit juice with water.
$39 \ \text{ml}$ of fruit juice is needed to make $600 \ \text{ml}$ of the drink.

How much water is needed make $800 \ \text{ml}$ of the drink?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A drink is made by mixing fruit juice with water.
$39 \ \text{ml}$ of fruit juice is needed to make $600 \ \text{ml}$ of the drink.
<emptyline>
How much water is needed make $800 \ \text{ml}$ of the drink?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using whole to part ratio,

$
\begin{array}{cccccccccccccc}
  & \text{drink}                       &:& \text{water} \\\\
= & 600 \ \text{ml}                    &:& (600 - 39) \ \text{ml} \\\\
= & 600                                &:& 561 \\\\
= & 1                                  &:& \dfrac {561} {600} \\\\
= & 800                                &:& \dfrac {561 \times 800} {600} \\\\
= & 800                                &:& \dfrac {3 \times 187 \times 2 \times 4 \times 100} {3 \times 2 \times 100} \\\\
= & 800                                &:& \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 187 \times \cancel 2 \times 4 \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel 3 \times \cancel 2 \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
= & 800                                &:& 187 \times 4 \\\\
= & 800 \ \text{ml}                    &:& 748 \ \text{ml} \\\\
\end{array}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$748 \ \text{ml}$

</div>
</div>

\end{array}
$
</code></pre>

</code></pre>

</li>
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In a maths test pupils have to answer all $20$ questions. $3$ marks are awarded for each correct answer, 
but $1$ mark is subtracted for each incorrect answer.

Bobby answers $4$ more questions correctly than Tim, and between them they score $48$ marks in total. 
How many questions did Tim answer correctly?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In a maths test pupils have to answer all $20$ questions. $3$ marks are awarded for each correct answer, 
but $1$ mark is subtracted for each incorrect answer.
<emptyline>
Bobby answers $4$ more questions correctly than Tim, and between them they score $48$ marks in total. 
How many questions did Tim answer correctly?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the number of correct answers by Tim $= t$

Incorrect answers by Tim $= 20 - t$

Number of correct answers by Bobby $= t + 4$

Incorrect answers by Bobby $= 20 - (t + 4)$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
3 \times t + 3 \times (t + 4) - (20 - t) - (20 - (t + 4))   &= 48 \\\\
3t + 3t + 12 - 20 + t -20 + t + 4                &= 48 \\\\
8t                               &= 48 - 12 + 20 + 20 - 4 \\\\
8t                                                          &= 72 \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {72} {8} \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {8 \times 9} {8} \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel 8 \times 9} {\cancel 8} \\\\
t                                                           &= 9
\end{aligned}
$

Number of correct answers by Tim $= 9$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the number of correct answers by Tim $= t$
<emptyline>
Incorrect answers by Tim $= 20 - t$
<emptyline>
Number of correct answers by Bobby $= t + 4$
<emptyline>
Incorrect answers by Bobby $= 20 - (t + 4)$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
3 \times t + 3 \times (t + 4) - (20 - t) - (20 - (t + 4))   &= 48 \\\\
3t + 3t + 12 - 20 + t -20 + t + 4                &= 48 \\\\
8t                               &= 48 - 12 + 20 + 20 - 4 \\\\
8t                                                          &= 72 \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {72} {8} \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {8 \times 9} {8} \\\\
t                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel 8 \times 9} {\cancel 8} \\\\
t                                                           &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
Number of correct answers by Tim $= 9$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In the mathematics puzzles below, the numbers in the square boxes are found by multiplying the two numbers in the circles it is 
joined to together.

Fill in all of the missing numbers in both the circles and the squares. The first puzzle has been solved for you as an example.

![papers/highgate-school/sample/sample-paper-c/s01-q03-s04-i00.png](/assets/papers/highgate-school/sample/sample-paper-c/s01-q03-s04-i00.png "papers/highgate-school/sample/sample-paper-c/s01-q03-s04-i00.png")



</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the mathematics puzzles below, the numbers in the square boxes are found by multiplying the two numbers in the circles it is 
joined to together.
<emptyline>
Fill in all of the missing numbers in both the circles and the squares. The first puzzle has been solved for you as an example.
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

TODO Make multiple sub subquestions if necessary.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Value in the first square}            &= 7 \times 3 \\\\
                                            &= 21 \\\\
\text{Value in the first circle}            &= \dfrac {18} {3} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {6 \times 3} {3} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel 3} {\cancel 3} \\\\
                                            &= 6 \\\\
\text{Value in the second square}           &= 7 \times 6 \\\\
                                            &= 42 \\\\
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the value in first circle $= a$

Value in second circle $= b$

Value in third circle $= c$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times b    &= 12 \\\\
b \times c    &= 20 \\\\
c \times a    &= 15
\end{aligned}
$

Let's multiply all the equation together to get the product of a, b and c.

$
\begin{aligned}
a \times b \times b \times c \times c \times a   &= 12 \times 20 \times 15 \\\\
a \times a \times b \times b \times c \times c   &= 3600 \\\\
a ^ 2 \times b ^ 2 \times c ^ 2                  &= 3600 \\\\
(a \times b \times c) ^ 2                        &= 3600 \\\\
a \times b \times c                              &= \sqrt {3600} \\\\
a \times b \times c                              &= 60
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the product of a \times b \times c  to find the value of c.

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a \times b \times c} {a \times b}                                   &= \dfrac {60} {12} \\\\
\dfrac {\cancel a \times \cancel b \times c} {\cancel a \times \cancel b}   &= \dfrac {12 \times 5} {12} \\\\
c                                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {12} \times 5} {\cancel {12}} \\\\
c                                                                           &= 5
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the product of a \times b \times c  to find the value of a.

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a \times b \times c} {b \times c}                                   &= \dfrac {60} {20} \\\\
\dfrac {a \times \cancel b \times \cancel c} {\cancel b \times \cancel c}   &= \dfrac {20 \times 3} {20} \\\\
a                                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times 3} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
a                                                                           &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

Let's use the product of a \times b \times c  to find the value of c.

$
\begin{aligned}
\dfrac {a \times b \times c} {c \times a}                                   &= \dfrac {60} {15} \\\\
\dfrac {\cancel a \times b \times \cancel c} {\cancel c \times \cancel a}   &= \dfrac {15 \times 4} {15} \\\\
c                                                                           &= \dfrac {\cancel {15} \times 4} {\cancel {15}} \\\\
c                                                                           &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

Value in first circle $= 3$.

Value in second circle $= 4$.

Value in third circle $= 5$.
</div>
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Value in the second square}           &= 7.5 \times 6 \\\\
                                            &= 45 \\\\
\text{Value in the first circle}            &= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {\cancel 3} {\cancel 3 \times 2} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
                                            &= 0.5 \\\\
\text{Value in the first square}            &= 7.5 \times 0.5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {75} {10} \times \dfrac {5} {10} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {75 \times 5} {100} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {375} {100} \\\\
                                            &= 3.75
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Value in first circle $= 6$

Value in first square $= 21$

Value in second square $= 42$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Value in first circle $= 3$

Value in second circle $= 4$

Value in third circle $= 5$

</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Value in first circle $= 0.5$

Value in first square $= 3.75$

Value in second square $= 45$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Two whole numbers multiply together to make $180$. What is the smallest possible difference between the two numbers?

- $3$
- $8$
- $11$
- $21$
- $57$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
- $3$
- $8$
- $11$
- $21$
- $57$
</code></pre>
</div>

We can easily infer the values by using trial and error. Lets find out $2$ numbers which meets the following critiera based on the equations above.

-The product of the $2$ numbers is $180$.

-The difference of the $2$ numbers should be minimum.

Factors of $180 = 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 9, 10, 12, 15, 18, 20, 30, 36, 45, 60, 90 \ \text{and} \ 180$ 

Factor pairs $= (1, 180) ,(2, 90) ,(3, 60), (4, 45), (5, 36), (6, 30) ,(9, 20), (10, 18), (12, 15) $    

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{The smallest difference of factor pairs}  &= 15 - 12 \\\\
                                                &= 3 
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Here's a rule to combine two numbers: multiply each number by itself, then add these two numbers together; finally add together the digits of this answer. 

For example $5$ and $8$ combine to give $17$ because $(5\times5)+(8\times8)=89$ and then $8+9=17$.

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What do you get when you combine $3$ and $7$ using this rule?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What do you get when you combine $3$ and $7$ using this rule?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Using the rule for numbers $3$ and $7$:

$\begin{aligned}
&= 3^2 + 7^2 \\\\
&= 9 + 49 \\\\
&= 58 \\\\
&= 5 + 8 \\\\
&= 13
\end{aligned}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I'm thinking of two different numbers. When I combine them using this rule, I get the answer $9$. What could my two numbers be?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I'm thinking of two different numbers. When I combine them using this rule, I get the answer $9$. What could my two numbers be?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the two numbers be $a$ and $b$.

According to the rule, we calculate $a^2 + b^2$, then sum the digits of the result to get $9$.

We can easily infer the values by using trial and error.

|  $N$ | Try $a^2 + b^2$                | $a \ne b$? |  Works? |
| :--: | :----------------------------- | :--------: | :-----: |
| $18$ | $3^2 + 3^2 = 9 + 9 = 18$       |     No     |    No   |
| $27$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $36$ | $6^2 + 0^2$ |     Yes     |    Yes   |
| $45$ | $6^2 + 3^2 = 36 + 9 = 45$      |     Yes    | Yes |
| $54$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $63$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $72$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $81$ | $9^2 + 0^2$      Yes     |    Yes   |
| $90$ | $9^2 + 3^2 = 81 + 9 = 90$      |     Yes    | Yes |
| $99$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |

A quick check shows $3$ and $6$ meets the criteria above.

Numbers $= 3, 6$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$3, 6$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the two numbers be $a$ and $b$.
<emptyline>
According to the rule, we calculate $a^2 + b^2$, then sum the digits of the result to get $9$.
<emptyline>
We can easily infer the values by using trial and error.
<emptyline>
|  $N$ | Try $a^2 + b^2$                | $a \ne b$? |  Works? |
| :--: | :----------------------------- | :--------: | :-----: |
| $18$ | $3^2 + 3^2 = 9 + 9 = 18$       |     No     |    No   |
| $27$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $36$ | $6^2 + 0^2$ |     Yes     |    Yes   |
| $45$ | $6^2 + 3^2 = 36 + 9 = 45$      |     Yes    | Yes |
| $54$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $63$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $72$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
| $81$ | $9^2 + 0^2$      Yes     |    Yes   |

| $99$ | No valid pair                  |      —     |    No   |
<emptyline>
A quick check shows $3$ and $6$ meets the criteria above.
<emptyline>
Numbers $= 3, 6$
</code></pre>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Three different positive odd numbers add up to $15$. What could the three numbers be?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Three different positive odd numbers add up to $15$. What could the three numbers be?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the three numbers $= a, \ a + 2, \ a + 4$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 2 + a + 4 &= 15 \\\\
3 a + 6           &= 15 \\\\
3 a               &= 15 - 6 \\\\
3 a               &= 9 \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {9} {3} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3} \\\\
a                 &= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {3}} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
a                 &= 3
\end{aligned}
$

Three numbers $= 3, \ 5, \ 7$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3, \ 5, \ 7$

</div>
</div>

Starting with the number $6$ the operation $\spadesuit \ 3$ is applied followed by the operation $\spadesuit \ 1$ to give a final answer of $1$, as in this example below:

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

$15 \quad \spadesuit \quad 3 \quad \spadesuit \quad 1 = {?}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$15 \quad \spadesuit \quad 3 \quad \spadesuit \quad 1 = {?}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \div 3 - 1 \\\\
&= \dfrac{15}{3} -1 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times 3}{3} -1 \\\\
&= \dfrac{5 \times \cancel 3}{\cancel 3} -1 \\\\
&= 5 - 1 \\\\
&= 4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>
